# One row per method that an agent unit rewrites to call back into the agent:
#   library version class method descriptor -> agent owner.method [xN] @ unit[,unit]
# xN is how many calls to that agent method the unit adds to the method (absent for one). A class under
# META-INF/versions/N/ of a multi-release JAR carries that prefix. A `# chain:` line is a backport
